- 5476 files · ~6,316,472 words

## Summary
- 60783 nodes · 193930 edges · 1585 communities (1258 shown, 327 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 23618 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness

- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
